- **mori-shokunin**: Fleet & host observability (森) — detect and report fleet liveness, wave settlement, kill/OOM events and resource runway. Triggers: 'fleet health', 'did a wave die', 'why did the daemon vanish', 'watchdog liveness', 'OOM kill'. Anti-trigger: NOT dispatch/orchestration (kumitate), NOT OS package/systemd/driver state (tsukuroi), NOT endpoint health (kama). Rank: shonen. ([VeigaPunk/mori-shokunin](https://github.com/VeigaPunk/mori-shokunin/tree/HEAD/.ufo/waves/round-42/assimilation/validate) · 2 copies)
- **moria**: Use when the user runs /moria [--scope mine|all] [--dry-run] [--auto] [--confirm]. Sweeps every repo in your global mend list (mend_repos.md) for open PRs/MRs bearing unaddressed comments — loops /durin --repo over each root in turn, then prints one combined report ending in a plain STIRRED/QUIET verdict. Mend follows comments across all your repos, on no project's schedule. The forge of each repo is auto-detected from its origin (github | gitlab). Per-repo behavior matches /durin exactly; one global list, set once. ([LarryHsiao/Skadi](https://github.com/LarryHsiao/Skadi/tree/HEAD/skills/moria))
